=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given integers N, M and a length-N sequence of non-negative integers A = (A_1, A_2, \ldots, A_N).
For k = 0, 1, \ldots, M-1, solve the following problem:

Define an integer sequence B = (B_1, B_2, \ldots, B_N) so that B_i is the remainder of A_i + k when divided by M. Find the inversion number in B.


What is the inversion number?
The inversion number of a sequence (A_1, A_2, \dots, A_N) is the number of integer pairs (i, j) satisfying 1 \le i < j \le N and A_i > A_j.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Print M lines.
The i-th line (1 \le i \le M) should contain the answer for the case k = i-1.

Constraints


- 1 \le N,M \le 2\times 10^5
- 0 \le A_i < M
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

3 3
2 1 0

Sample Output 1

3
1
1


- For k=0: B=(2, 1, 0). The inversion number is 3.
- For k=1: B=(0, 2, 1). The inversion number is 1.
- For k=2: B=(1, 0, 2). The inversion number is 1.

Sample Input 2

5 6
5 3 5 0 1

Sample Output 2

7
3
3
1
1
5

Sample Input 3

7 7
0 1 2 3 4 5 6

Sample Output 3

0
6
10
12
12
10
6

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Can express in terms of circular order. For fixed pair, as k increases by 1 mod M, values both increment, wrap at M. Relative order changes only when one of x+k or y+k crosses M (wraps), i.e., when k = M - x or M - y (mod M). At that k, one value becomes 0 and the other maybe near M? Need.

Let's derive exact intervals. Let x,y distinct? If x=y, contribution always 0 because equal after shift. So only pairs with different values.

So for x<y, inversion occurs iff y+k ≥ M and x+k < M, i.e. M-y ≤ k < M-x. Since k in 0..M-1. This interval length y-x. If M-y <= M-x? yes.

If x>y, by symmetry? Let's set original a > b. Then without wrap a+k > b+k, inversion true. When a wraps but b not, a' < b', no inversion. When both wrap, a'>b' true? Let's be absolutely sure about update formula with "value M-1" only. Let's formalize:

Let C(k) = (A+k) mod M elementwise. Let C(k+1) = (C(k)+1) mod M. For any pair i<j:
- If neither C_i nor C_j is M-1: C_i+1 > C_j+1 iff C_i > C_j, so unchanged.
- If C_i = C_j = M-1: both become 0, equality, unchanged (no inversion before/after).
- If C_i = M-1, C_j < M-1: before C_i > C_j (true); after C_i'=0, C_j'=C_j+1≥1, so false. Thus inversion removed for every such pair.
- If C_i < M-1, C_j = M-1: before false; after C_i'≥1, C_j'=0, true. Thus inversion added for every such pair.
So delta = #(i<j, C_i<M-1, C_j=M-1) - #(i<j, C_i=M-1, C_j<M-1). Since C_i=M-1 iff A_i = M-1-k = v. Thus delta_v = R_v - L_v. Good.

Now formula for R_v/L_v:
R_v = # pairs (i<j, A_i≠v, A_j=v). For each occurrence j of v, number of earlier positions not v = (j-1) - (# occurrences of v before j). If p_t is position, t-th occurrence, earlier v count t-1, so R_t = p_t - t. Sum R = Σ(p_t - t).
L_v = # pairs (i<j, A_i=v, A_j≠v). For each occurrence i=p_t, later positions not v = (N-p_t) - (c-t). Sum L = Σ(N-p_t - c + t).
delta = Σ[(p_t - t) - (N-p_t - c + t)] = Σ[2p_t - N - c - 2t]? Wait earlier I wrote N-p-c+t. Let's recalc L_t = (N-p_t) - (c-t) = N - p_t - c + t. R_t - L_t = p_t - t - (N - p_t - c + t) = 2p_t - N - c - 2t? Wait p_t - t - N + p_t + c - t = 2p_t - N + c - 2t. Yes, not -c? Let's check: -t - (+t) = -2t; - (N - p_t - c + t) = -N + p_t + c - t. Sum p_t - t -N + p_t + c - t = 2p_t - N + c - 2t. I earlier wrote 2p - N + c - 2t. Good. Sum over t: 2Σp - cN + c^2 - 2*c(c+1)/2 = 2Σp - cN + c^2 - c(c+1) = 2Σp - cN - c = 2Σp - c(N+1). Yes. Good.

Let's think about memory and speed. Python code:
import sys
def main():
    input=sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N,M=map(int,input().split())
    A=list(map(int,input().split()))
    bit=[0]*(M+1)
    def add(i, v):
        while i <= M:
            bit[i]+=v
            i += i & -i
    def sum_(i):
        s=0
        while i>0:
            s+=bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        return s
    inv=0
    seen=0
    cnt=[0]*M
    pos_sum=[0]*M
    for idx,a in enumerate(A,1):
        cnt[a]+=1
        pos_sum[a]+=idx
        # Fenwick index a+1
        le = sum_(a+1)
        inv += seen - le
        add(a+1,1)
        seen += 1
    delta=[0]*M
    Np1=N+1
    for v in range(M):
        delta[v] = 2*pos_sum[v] - cnt[v]*Np1
    ans=[0]*M
    ans[0]=inv
    for k in range(M-1):
        v = M - 1 - k
        # since k 0..M-2, v 1..M-1? Wait M-1-k ranges M-1 down to 1, no need mod. Yes if k<=M-2, v>=1. So v=M-1-k.
        ans[k+1] = ans[k] + delta[v]
    sys.stdout.write('\n'.join(map(str, ans)))
if __name__ == "__main__": main()

Note: v = M-1-k. For k=M-2, v=1. For k=0, v=M-1. So no modulo needed. But if M=1, loop not run. Good. But if we use modulo, also fine. Simpler no mod.

Need ensure delta[v] for v=M-1-k exists: v in 1..M-1. We never use delta[0] in forward loop. But ans0 computed. If M=1, ans[0] only. Good.

Let's write:
def main():
    import sys
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N, M = data[0], data[1]
    A = data[2:2+N]
    bit = [0]*(M+1)
    cnt = [0]*M
    pos_sum = [0]*M
    inv = 0
    seen = 0
    for idx, a in enumerate(A, 1):
        cnt[a] += 1
        pos_sum[a] += idx
        # query prefix up to a+1
        s = 0
        i = a + 1
        while i > 0:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        inv += seen - s
        i = a + 1
        while i <= M:
            bit[i] += 1
            i += i & -i
        seen += 1
    Np1 = N + 1
    delta = [0]*M
    for v in range(M):
        delta[v] = 2 * pos_sum[v] - cnt[v] * Np1
    ans = [0]*M
    ans[0] = inv
    for k in range(M - 1):
        v = M - 1 - k
        ans[k+1] = ans[k] + delta[v]
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

Let's maybe derive delta_v from R-L in terms of count and sum positions in another way to be absolutely certain:
R_v = sum over occurrences p_t of (# earlier positions with value != v) = Σ(p_t - 1 - (t-1)) = Σ(p_t - t). Yes.

First term = Σp_t - Σt.

Let's write final code:

import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N, M = data[0], data[1]

    bit = [0] * (M + 1)
    cnt = [0] * M
    pos_sum = [0] * M

    inv = 0
    seen = 0

    for pos in range(1, N + 1):
        a = data[pos + 1]
        cnt[a] += 1
        pos_sum[a] += pos

        # count previous elements > a
        s = 0
        i = a + 1
        while i > 0:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        inv += seen - s

        i = a + 1
        while i <= M:
            bit[i] += 1
            i += i & -i
        seen += 1

    del data

    delta = [0] * M
    np1 = N + 1
    for v in range(M):
        delta[v] = 2 * pos_sum[v] - cnt[v] * np1

    ans = [0] * M
    ans[0] = inv
    for k in range(M - 1):
        v = M - 1 - k
        ans[k + 1] = ans[k] + delta[v]

    print("\n".join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Now, let's maybe prove the update formula in terms of original value v:
At step from k to k+1, the elements that become 0 are those with current value M-1. Current value = (A_i+k) mod M = M-1 iff A_i = M-1-k mod M. Let v = M-1-k. Then:
- Pairs that lose inversion: current first value M-1, current second value < M-1. In original terms, first A_i=v, second A_j≠v. Count L_v.
- Pairs that gain inversion: current first value < M-1, current second value M-1. In original terms, first A_i≠v, second A_j=v. Count R_v.
Thus delta=R_v-L_v. Formula. Good.

Let's think if there is any issue with pairs where current second value M-1 and current first value also M-1? They are equal, not counted in gain because first value < M-1 false. Not counted in loss because second value < M-1 false. Good.

If original different, after shift different. So no new equalities except original equal. Our update formula handles equal original M-1 pairs as unchanged. Good.

Let's write final code with no delta array:

import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]; M = data[1]
    bit = [0]*(M+1)
    cnt = [0]*M
    pos_sum = [0]*M
    inv = 0
    seen = 0
    for pos in range(1, N+1):
        a = data[pos+1]
        cnt[a] += 1
        pos_sum[a] += pos

        s = 0
        i = a + 1
        while i:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i
        inv += seen - s

        i = a + 1
        while i <= M:
            bit[i] += 1
            i += i & -i
        seen += 1

    # no need data
    res = [str(inv)]
    cur = inv
    np1 = N + 1
    for k in range(M - 1):
        v = M - 1 - k
        cur += 2 * pos_sum[v] - cnt[v] * np1
        res.append(str(cur))
    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(res))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N, M = data[0], data[1]

    bit = [0] * (M + 1)
    cnt = [0] * M
    pos_sum = [0] * M

    inv = 0
    seen = 0

    for pos in range(1, N + 1):
        a = data[pos + 1]

        cnt[a] += 1
        pos_sum[a] += pos

        # Count previous elements greater than a.
        s = 0
        i = a + 1
        while i > 0:
            s += bit[i]
            i -= i & -i

        inv += seen - s

        i = a + 1
        while i <= M:
            bit[i] += 1
            i += i & -i

        seen += 1

    res = [str(inv)]
    cur = inv
    np1 = N + 1

    for k in range(M - 1):
        v = M - 1 - k
        cur += 2 * pos_sum[v] - cnt[v] * np1
        res.append(str(cur))

    sys.stdout.write("\n".join(res))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
